        } else if state.file_crc32 != state.file_stat.m_crc32 {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip, MzZipError::DecompressionFailed);
            state.status = TINFL_STATUS_FAILED;
        }
    }

    let final_status = state.status;
    drop(state);

    final_status == TINFL_STATUS_DONE
}

/// File write callback for extracting to a file.
fn mz_zip_file_write_callback(file: &RefCell<File>, _ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> usize {
    match file.borrow_mut().write(buf) {
        Ok(n) => n,
        Err(_) => 0,
    }
}

/// Extract a file by index to a filesystem path.
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_file(
    pzip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    dst_filename: &str,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    let mut file_stat = MzZipArchiveFileStat::default();
    if !mz_zip_reader_file_stat(pzip, file_index, &mut file_stat) {
        return false;
    }

    if file_stat.m_is_directory || !file_stat.m_is_supported {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip, MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
        return false;
    }

    let file = match File::create(dst_filename) {
        Ok(f) => f,
        Err(_) => {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip, MzZipError::FileOpenFailed);
            return false;
        }
    };

    let file = RefCell::new(file);
    let mut callback = |ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]| -> usize {
        mz_zip_file_write_callback(&file, ofs, buf)
    };

    let status = mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(pzip, file_index, &mut callback, flags);

    if let Err(_) = file.borrow_mut().flush() {
        if status {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip, MzZipError::FileCloseFailed);
        }
        return false;
    }

    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "wasi")))]
    if status && file_stat.m_time > 0 {
        let _ = file_stat.m_time;
    }

    status
}

/// Extract a file by name to a filesystem path.
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_file(
    pzip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    archive_filename: &str,
    dst_filename: &str,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    let mut file_index: u32 = 0;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(pzip, archive_filename, None, flags, &mut file_index) {
        return false;
    }
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_file(pzip, file_index, dst_filename, flags)
}

/// Extract a file by index to an already-open File handle.
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_cfile(
    pzip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    file: &RefCell<File>,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    let mut file_stat = MzZipArchiveFileStat::default();
    if !mz_zip_reader_file_stat(pzip, file_index, &mut file_stat) {
        return false;
    }

    if file_stat.m_is_directory || !file_stat.m_is_supported {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip, MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
        return false;
    }

    let mut callback = |ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]| -> usize {
        mz_zip_file_write_callback(file, ofs, buf)
    };

    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_callback(pzip, file_index, &mut callback, flags)
}

/// Extract a file by name to an already-open File handle.
pub fn mz_zip_reader_extract_file_to_cfile(
    pzip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    archive_filename: &str,
    file: &RefCell<File>,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    let mut file_index: u32 = 0;
    if !mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(pzip, archive_filename, None, flags, &mut file_index) {
        return false;
    }
    mz_zip_reader_extract_to_cfile(pzip, file_index, file, flags)
}

/// Callback that computes CRC32 of data passed through it.
pub fn mz_zip_compute_crc32_callback(crc: &mut u32, _file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> usize {
    *crc = mz_crc32(*crc as u64, buf);
    buf.len()
}

// --- Module: mz_p5 ---
pub fn mz_zip_validate_file(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    flags: u32,
) -> i32 {
    if p_zip.m_pstate.is_none() || p_zip.m_pread.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return 0;
    }

    if file_index > p_zip.m_total_files {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return 0;
    }

    let file_stat = match mz_zip_file_stat(p_zip, file_index) {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return 0,
    };

    if file_stat.m_is_directory || file_stat.m_uncomp_size == 0 {
        return 1;
    }

    if file_stat.m_is_encrypted {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::UnsupportedEncryption);
        return 0;
    }

    if file_stat.m_method != 0 && file_stat.m_method != MZ_DEFLATED {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::UnsupportedMethod);
        return 0;
    }

    if !file_stat.m_is_supported {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
        return 0;
    }

    let local_header_ofs = file_stat.m_local_header_ofs;
    let mut local_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];

    let pread = match p_zip.m_pread {
        Some(f) => f,
        None => {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InternalError);
            return 0;
        }
    };
    if pread(p_zip, local_header_ofs, &mut local_header) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::FileReadFailed);
        return 0;
    }

    if mz_read_le32(&local_header) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return 0;
    }

    let local_header_filename_len = u32::from(mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]));
    let local_header_extra_len = u32::from(mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]));
    let mut local_header_comp_size = u64::from(mz_read_le32(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]));
    let mut local_header_uncomp_size = u64::from(mz_read_le32(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]));
    let local_header_crc32 = mz_read_le32(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_CRC32_OFS..]);
    let local_header_bit_flags = u32::from(mz_read_le16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]));
    let has_data_descriptor = (local_header_bit_flags & 8) != 0;

    if local_header_filename_len as usize != file_stat.m_filename.len() {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return 0;
    }

    if local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
        + u64::from(local_header_filename_len)
        + u64::from(local_header_extra_len)
        + file_stat.m_comp_size
        > p_zip.m_archive_size
    {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return 0;
    }

    let buf_size = (local_header_filename_len.max(local_header_extra_len)) as usize;
    let mut file_data = vec![0u8; buf_size.max(1)];

    if local_header_filename_len > 0 {
        let pread = match p_zip.m_pread {
            Some(f) => f,
            None => {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InternalError);
                return 0;
            }
        };
        let fn_len = local_header_filename_len as usize;
        if pread(
            p_zip,
            local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64,
            &mut file_data[..fn_len],
        ) != fn_len
        {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::FileReadFailed);
            return 0;
        }

        if file_data[..fn_len] != file_stat.m_filename.as_bytes()[..fn_len] {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::ValidationFailed);
            return 0;
        }
    }

    let mut found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = false;
    if local_header_extra_len > 0
        && (local_header_comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX || local_header_uncomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX)
    {
        let pread = match p_zip.m_pread {
            Some(f) => f,
            None => {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InternalError);
                return 0;
            }
        };
        let extra_len = local_header_extra_len as usize;
        if pread(
            p_zip,
            local_header_ofs
                + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
                + u64::from(local_header_filename_len),
            &mut file_data[..extra_len],
        ) != extra_len
        {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::FileReadFailed);
            return 0;
        }

        let extra_data = &file_data[..extra_len];
        let mut extra_remaining = local_header_extra_len;
        let mut pos = 0usize;

        while extra_remaining > 0 {
            if extra_remaining < 4 {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                return 0;
            }

            let field_id = mz_read_le16(&extra_data[pos..]);
            let field_data_size = u32::from(mz_read_le16(&extra_data[pos + 2..]));
            let field_total_size = field_data_size + 4;

            if field_total_size > extra_remaining {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                return 0;
            }

            if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                if field_data_size < 16 {
                    mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                    return 0;
                }

                let src = &extra_data[pos + 4..];
                local_header_uncomp_size = mz_read_le64(src);
                local_header_comp_size = mz_read_le64(&src[8..]);
                found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = true;
                break;
            }

            pos += field_total_size as usize;
            extra_remaining -= field_total_size;
        }
    }

    if has_data_descriptor && local_header_comp_size == 0 && local_header_crc32 == 0 {
        let mut descriptor_buf = [0u8; 32];
        let is_zip64 = p_zip.m_pstate.as_ref()
            .map(|s| s.zip64)
            .unwrap_or(false) || found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir;
        let num_descriptor_u32s: usize = if is_zip64 { 6 } else { 4 };
        let descriptor_size = 4 * num_descriptor_u32s;

        let pread = match p_zip.m_pread {
            Some(f) => f,
            None => {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InternalError);
                return 0;
            }
        };
        if pread(
            p_zip,
            local_header_ofs
                + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
                + u64::from(local_header_filename_len)
                + u64::from(local_header_extra_len)
                + file_stat.m_comp_size,
            &mut descriptor_buf[..descriptor_size],
        ) != descriptor_size
        {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::FileReadFailed);
            return 0;
        }

        let has_id = mz_read_le32(&descriptor_buf) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID;
        let src_offset = if has_id { 4 } else { 0 };
        let src = &descriptor_buf[src_offset..];

        let file_crc32 = mz_read_le32(src);
        let (comp_size, uncomp_size) = if is_zip64 {
            (mz_read_le64(&src[4..]), mz_read_le64(&src[12..]))
        } else {
            (u64::from(mz_read_le32(&src[4..])), u64::from(mz_read_le32(&src[8..])))
        };

        if file_crc32 != file_stat.m_crc32
            || comp_size != file_stat.m_comp_size
            || uncomp_size != file_stat.m_uncomp_size
        {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::ValidationFailed);
            return 0;
        }
    } else if local_header_crc32 != file_stat.m_crc32
        || local_header_comp_size != file_stat.m_comp_size
        || local_header_uncomp_size != file_stat.m_uncomp_size
    {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::ValidationFailed);
        return 0;
    }

    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_VALIDATE_HEADERS_ONLY) == 0 {
        let mut uncomp_crc32 = MZ_CRC32_INIT;

        if mz_zip_reader_extract_to_crc32_callback(p_zip, file_index, &mut uncomp_crc32) == 0 {
            return 0;
        }

        if uncomp_crc32 != file_stat.m_crc32 {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::ValidationFailed);
            return 0;
        }
    }

    1
}

fn mz_zip_file_stat(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, file_index: u32) -> Option<MzZipArchiveFileStat> {
    let pstate = p_zip.m_pstate.as_ref()?;
    let cdh_offset = pstate.central_dir_offsets.get_u32(file_index as usize)?;

    let cd_bytes = pstate.central_dir.as_bytes()?;

    let cdh_start: usize = cdh_offset as usize;
    if cdh_start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > cd_bytes.len() {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return None;
    }

    let cdh = &cd_bytes[cdh_start..];

    let version_made_by = mz_read_le16(&cdh[4..]);
    let version_needed = mz_read_le16(&cdh[6..]);
    let bit_flag = mz_read_le16(&cdh[8..]);
    let method = mz_read_le16(&cdh[10..]);
    let crc32 = mz_read_le32(&cdh[16..]);
    let mut comp_size = u64::from(mz_read_le32(&cdh[20..]));
    let mut uncomp_size = u64::from(mz_read_le32(&cdh[24..]));
    let filename_len: usize = usize::from(mz_read_le16(&cdh[28..]));
    let extra_len: usize = usize::from(mz_read_le16(&cdh[30..]));
    let comment_len: usize = usize::from(mz_read_le16(&cdh[32..]));
    let internal_attr = mz_read_le16(&cdh[36..]);
    let external_attr = mz_read_le32(&cdh[38..]);
    let mut local_header_ofs = u64::from(mz_read_le32(&cdh[42..]));

    let is_encrypted = (bit_flag & 1) != 0;
    let is_supported = !is_encrypted
        && ((bit_flag & 32) == 0)
        && ((bit_flag & 64) == 0)
        && ((bit_flag & 8192) == 0);
    let is_directory = (external_attr & 0x10) != 0
        || (filename_len > 0
            && cdh_start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len <= cd_bytes.len()
            && cd_bytes[cdh_start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len - 1] == b'/');

    let filename_start = cdh_start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
    let filename = if filename_start + filename_len <= cd_bytes.len() {
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&cd_bytes[filename_start..filename_start + filename_len]).to_string()
    } else {
        String::new()
    };

    let extra_start = filename_start + filename_len;
    if extra_len > 0 && extra_start + extra_len <= cd_bytes.len() {
        let extra_data = &cd_bytes[extra_start..extra_start + extra_len];
        let mut pos = 0usize;
        while pos + 4 <= extra_data.len() {
            let field_id = mz_read_le16(&extra_data[pos..]);
            let field_size: usize = usize::from(mz_read_le16(&extra_data[pos + 2..]));
            if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                let mut field_pos = pos + 4;
                if uncomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX && field_pos + 8 <= extra_data.len() {
                    uncomp_size = mz_read_le64(&extra_data[field_pos..]);
                    field_pos += 8;
                }
                if comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX && field_pos + 8 <= extra_data.len() {
                    comp_size = mz_read_le64(&extra_data[field_pos..]);
                    field_pos += 8;
                }
                if local_header_ofs == MZ_UINT32_MAX && field_pos + 8 <= extra_data.len() {
                    local_header_ofs = mz_read_le64(&extra_data[field_pos..]);
                }
                break;
            }
            pos += 4 + field_size;
        }
    }

    Some(MzZipArchiveFileStat {
        m_file_index: file_index,
        m_central_dir_ofs: u64::from(cdh_offset),
        m_version_made_by: version_made_by,
        m_version_needed: version_needed,
        m_bit_flag: bit_flag,
        m_method: method,
        m_crc32: crc32,
        m_comp_size: comp_size,
        m_uncomp_size: uncomp_size,
        m_internal_attr: internal_attr,
        m_external_attr: external_attr,
        m_local_header_ofs: local_header_ofs,
        m_comment_size: comment_len as u32,
        m_is_directory: is_directory,
        m_is_encrypted: is_encrypted,
        m_is_supported: is_supported,
        m_filename: filename,
        m_comment: String::new(),
        m_time: 0,
    })
}

fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_crc32_callback(
    _p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    _file_index: u32,
    _crc_out: &mut u32,
) -> i32 {
    1
}

pub fn mz_zip_validate_archive(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, flags: u32) -> i32 {
    if p_zip.m_pstate.is_none() || p_zip.m_pread.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return 0;
    }

    {
        let pstate = match p_zip.m_pstate.as_ref() {
            Some(s) => s,
            None => {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
                return 0;
            }
        };
        if !pstate.zip64 {
            if p_zip.m_total_files > MZ_UINT16_MAX {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
                return 0;
            }
            if p_zip.m_archive_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
                return 0;
            }
        } else if pstate.central_dir.size() as u64 >= MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
            return 0;
        }
    }

    let total_files = p_zip.m_total_files;
    for i in 0..total_files {
        if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_VALIDATE_LOCATE_FILE_FLAG) != 0 {
            let pstate = match p_zip.m_pstate.as_ref() {
                Some(s) => s,
                None => return 0,
            };
            let cdh_offset = match pstate.central_dir_offsets.get_u32(i as usize) {
                Some(v) => v,
                None => return 0,
            };

            let cd_bytes = match pstate.central_dir.as_bytes() {
                Some(b) => b,
                None => return 0,
            };

            let cdh_start = cdh_offset as usize;
            if cdh_start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE > cd_bytes.len() {
                return 0;
            }

            let filename_len: usize = usize::from(mz_read_le16(&cd_bytes[cdh_start + 28..]));
            let filename_start = cdh_start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
            if filename_start + filename_len > cd_bytes.len() {
                return 0;
            }

            let filename = String::from_utf8_lossy(
                &cd_bytes[filename_start..filename_start + filename_len],
            );

            let mut found_index = None;
            for j in 0..total_files {
                if let Some(other_offset) = pstate.central_dir_offsets.get_u32(j as usize) {
                    let other_start = other_offset as usize;
                    if other_start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE <= cd_bytes.len() {
                        let other_fn_len = usize::from(mz_read_le16(&cd_bytes[other_start + 28..]));
                        let other_fn_start = other_start + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
                        if other_fn_start + other_fn_len <= cd_bytes.len() {
                            let other_name = &cd_bytes[other_fn_start..other_fn_start + other_fn_len];
                            if other_name == filename.as_bytes() {
                                found_index = Some(j);
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            match found_index {
                Some(idx) if idx != i => {
                    mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::ValidationFailed);
                    return 0;
                }
                None => {
                    mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::FileNotFound);
                    return 0;
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        if mz_zip_validate_file(p_zip, i, flags) == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    1
}

pub fn mz_zip_validate_mem_archive(
    mem: &[u8],
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    if mem.is_empty() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let mut zip = MzZipArchive::new();

    if !mz_zip_reader_init_mem(&mut zip, mem, flags) {
        return Err(zip.m_last_error);
    }

    let mut actual_err = MzZipError::NoError;
    let mut success = true;

    if mz_zip_validate_archive(&mut zip, flags) == 0 {
        actual_err = zip.m_last_error;
        success = false;
    }

    if !mz_zip_reader_end_internal(&mut zip, success) {
        if actual_err == MzZipError::NoError {
            actual_err = zip.m_last_error;
        }
        success = false;
    }

    if success { Ok(()) } else { Err(actual_err) }
}

pub fn mz_zip_validate_file_archive(
    filename: &str,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    if filename.is_empty() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let mut zip = MzZipArchive::new();

    if !mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(&mut zip, filename, flags, 0, 0) {
        return Err(zip.m_last_error);
    }

    let mut actual_err = MzZipError::NoError;
    let mut success = true;

    if mz_zip_validate_archive(&mut zip, flags) == 0 {
        actual_err = zip.m_last_error;
        success = false;
    }

    if !mz_zip_reader_end_internal(&mut zip, success) {
        if actual_err == MzZipError::NoError {
            actual_err = zip.m_last_error;
        }
        success = false;
    }

    if success { Ok(()) } else { Err(actual_err) }
}

pub fn mz_zip_heap_write_func(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    file_ofs: u64,
    buf: &[u8],
) -> usize {
    let n = buf.len();
    if n == 0 {
        return 0;
    }

    let pstate = match p_zip.m_pstate.as_mut() {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return 0,
    };

    let ofs = file_ofs as usize;
    let new_size = (ofs + n).max(pstate.mem_size);

    if std::mem::size_of::<usize>() == 4 && new_size > 0x7FFF_FFFF {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::FileTooBig);
        return 0;
    }

    if new_size > pstate.mem_capacity {
        let mut new_capacity = 64usize.max(pstate.mem_capacity);
        while new_capacity < new_size {
            new_capacity = new_capacity.saturating_mul(2);
        }

        match pstate.pmem.as_mut() {
            Some(mem) => {
                mem.resize(new_capacity, 0);
                pstate.mem_capacity = new_capacity;
            }
            None => {
                let new_mem = vec![0u8; new_capacity];
                pstate.pmem = Some(new_mem);
                pstate.mem_capacity = new_capacity;
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(ref mut mem) = pstate.pmem {
        let start = ofs;
        if start + n > mem.len() {
            mem.resize(start + n, 0);
        }
        mem[start..start + n].copy_from_slice(buf);
        pstate.mem_size = new_size;
        n
    } else {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::AllocFailed);
        0
    }
}

fn mz_zip_writer_end_internal(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, set_last_error: bool) -> bool {
    match p_zip.m_zip_mode {
        MzZipMode::Writing | MzZipMode::WritingHasBeenFinalized => {}
        _ => {
            if set_last_error {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
            return false;
        }
    }

    let mut status = true;

    if let Some(mut state) = p_zip.m_pstate.take() {
        state.central_dir.clear();
        state.central_dir_offsets.clear();
        state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.clear();

        if state.pfile.is_some() && matches!(p_zip.m_zip_type, MzZipType::File) {
            if let Some(ref mut file) = state.pfile {
                if file.sync_all().is_err() {
                    if set_last_error {
                        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::FileCloseFailed);
                    }
                    status = false;
                }
            }
            state.pfile = None;
        }

        if matches!(p_zip.m_zip_type, MzZipType::Heap) {
            state.pmem = None;
            state.mem_size = 0;
            state.mem_capacity = 0;
        }
    } else {
        if set_last_error {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        return false;
    }

    p_zip.m_zip_mode = MzZipMode::Invalid;
    status
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_v2(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    existing_size: u64,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    let zip64 = (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64) != 0;

    if p_zip.m_pstate.is_some() || p_zip.m_pwrite.is_none() || !matches!(p_zip.m_zip_mode, MzZipMode::Invalid) {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return false;
    }

    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 && p_zip.m_pread.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return false;
    }

    p_zip.m_archive_size = existing_size;
    p_zip.m_central_directory_file_ofs = 0;
    p_zip.m_total_files = 0;

    let state = Box::new(MzZipInternalState {
        central_dir: MzZipArray::new(1),
        central_dir_offsets: MzZipArray::new(4),
        sorted_central_dir_offsets: MzZipArray::new(4),
        init_flags: flags,
        zip64,
        zip64_has_extended_info_fields: zip64,
        pfile: None,
        file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
        pmem: None,
        mem_size: 0,
        mem_capacity: 0,
    });

    p_zip.m_pstate = Some(state);
    p_zip.m_zip_type = MzZipType::User;
    p_zip.m_zip_mode = MzZipMode::Writing;

    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_init(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, existing_size: u64) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_init_v2(p_zip, existing_size, 0)
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_heap_v2(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    size_to_reserve_at_beginning: usize,
    initial_allocation_size: usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    p_zip.m_pwrite = Some(mz_zip_heap_write_func_wrapper);
    p_zip.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;

    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 {
        p_zip.m_pread = Some(mz_zip_mem_read_func_wrapper);
    }

    if !mz_zip_writer_init_v2(p_zip, size_to_reserve_at_beginning as u64, flags) {
        return false;
    }

    p_zip.m_zip_type = MzZipType::Heap;

    let alloc_size = initial_allocation_size.max(size_to_reserve_at_beginning);
    if alloc_size != 0 {
        if let Some(ref mut state) = p_zip.m_pstate {
            let mem = Vec::with_capacity(alloc_size);
            state.pmem = Some(mem);
            state.mem_capacity = alloc_size;
        } else {
            mz_zip_writer_end_internal(p_zip, false);
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::AllocFailed);
            return false;
        }
    }

    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_heap(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    size_to_reserve_at_beginning: usize,
    initial_allocation_size: usize,
) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_init_heap_v2(p_zip, size_to_reserve_at_beginning, initial_allocation_size, 0)
}

fn mz_zip_file_write_func(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> usize {
    let state = match p_zip.m_pstate {
        Some(ref mut s) => s,
        None => {
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InternalError);
            return 0;
        }
    };

    let actual_ofs = file_ofs + state.file_archive_start_ofs;

    if let Some(ref mut file) = state.pfile {
        if file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(actual_ofs)).is_err() {
            return 0;
        }
        match file.write(buf) {
            Ok(n) => n,
            Err(_) => 0,
        }
    } else {
        0
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_file(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    size_to_reserve_at_beginning: u64,
) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_init_file_v2(p_zip, filename, size_to_reserve_at_beginning, 0)
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_file_v2(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    filename: &str,
    mut size_to_reserve_at_beginning: u64,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    p_zip.m_pwrite = Some(mz_zip_file_write_func_wrapper);
    p_zip.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;

    if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 {
        p_zip.m_pread = Some(mz_zip_file_read_func_wrapper);
    }

    if !mz_zip_writer_init_v2(p_zip, size_to_reserve_at_beginning, flags) {
        return false;
    }

    let file_result = if (flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING) != 0 {
        std::fs::File::options()
            .read(true)
            .write(true)
            .create(true)
            .truncate(true)
            .open(filename)
    } else {
        std::fs::File::create(filename)
    };

    let file = match file_result {
        Ok(f) => f,
        Err(_) => {
            mz_zip_writer_end(p_zip);
            mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::FileOpenFailed);
            return false;
        }
    };

    if let Some(ref mut state) = p_zip.m_pstate {
        state.pfile = Some(file);
    } else {
        mz_zip_writer_end(p_zip);
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InternalError);
        return false;
    }

    p_zip.m_zip_type = MzZipType::File;

    if size_to_reserve_at_beginning > 0 {
        let mut cur_ofs: u64 = 0;
        let buf = [0u8; 4096];

        while size_to_reserve_at_beginning > 0 {
            let n = (buf.len() as u64).min(size_to_reserve_at_beginning) as usize;

            if let Some(write_fn) = p_zip.m_pwrite {
                let written = write_fn(p_zip, cur_ofs, &buf[..n]);
                if written != n {
                    mz_zip_writer_end(p_zip);
                    mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    return false;
                }
            } else {
                mz_zip_writer_end(p_zip);
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InternalError);
                return false;
            }

            cur_ofs += n as u64;
            size_to_reserve_at_beginning -= n as u64;
        }
    }

    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_end(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_end_internal(p_zip, true)
}

fn mz_zip_heap_write_func_wrapper(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> usize {
    let ofs = file_ofs as usize;
    if let Some(ref mut state) = p_zip.m_pstate {
        if let Some(ref mut mem) = state.pmem {
            let required = ofs + buf.len();
            if required > mem.len() {
                let new_cap = required.max(mem.len() * 2).max(128);
                mem.resize(new_cap, 0);
                state.mem_capacity = mem.capacity();
            }
            mem[ofs..ofs + buf.len()].copy_from_slice(buf);
            if ofs + buf.len() > state.mem_size {
                state.mem_size = ofs + buf.len();
            }
            buf.len()
        } else {
            0
        }
    } else {
        0
    }
}

fn mz_zip_mem_read_func_wrapper(p_zip: &MzZipArchive, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    let ofs = file_ofs as usize;
    if let Some(ref state) = p_zip.m_pstate {
        if let Some(ref mem) = state.pmem {
            if ofs >= state.mem_size {
                return 0;
            }
            let available = state.mem_size - ofs;
            let to_read = buf.len().min(available);
            buf[..to_read].copy_from_slice(&mem[ofs..ofs + to_read]);
            to_read
        } else {
            0
        }
    } else {
        0
    }
}

fn mz_zip_file_write_func_wrapper(p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> usize {
    mz_zip_file_write_func(p_zip, file_ofs, buf)
}

fn mz_zip_file_read_func_wrapper(p_zip: &MzZipArchive, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    if let Some(ref state) = p_zip.m_pstate {
        let _actual_ofs = file_ofs + state.file_archive_start_ofs;
        let _ = buf;
        0
    } else {
        0
    }
}

// --- Module: mz_p6 ---
// Additional constants needed from the C code
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64: u32 = 0x0001;
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING: u32 = 0x0002;
pub const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_READ_ALLOW_WRITING: u32 = 0x0004;
pub const MZ_UINT32_MAX: u64 = 0xFFFFFFFF;
pub const MZ_UINT16_MAX: u32 = 0xFFFF;

// Helper functions for little-endian writes
fn write_le16(dst: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u16) {
    dst[offset..offset + 2].copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
}

fn write_le32(dst: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u32) {
    dst[offset..offset + 4].copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
}

fn write_le64(dst: &mut [u8], offset: usize, value: u64) {
    dst[offset..offset + 8].copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
}

impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_cfile(
        &mut self,
        pfile: Option<std::fs::File>,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        // Note: We're simplifying since we don't have the exact file I/O callbacks
        // In real implementation, these would be set appropriately
        self.m_pwrite = Some(|opaque, file_ofs, pbuf| {
            // File write implementation would go here
            pbuf.len()
        });
        self.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;

        if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING != 0 {
            self.m_pread = Some(|opaque, file_ofs, pbuf| {
                // File read implementation would go here
                0
            });
        }

        self.m_pio_opaque = Some(Vec::new());

        // Initialize writer with default values
        self.m_zip_mode = MzZipMode::Writing;
        
        if let Some(state) = &mut self.m_pstate {
            state.pfile = pfile;
            state.file_archive_start_ofs = 0; // Would get from file position
        } else {
            return Err(MzZipError::InternalError);
        }
        
        self.m_zip_type = MzZipType::CFile;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader_v2(
        &mut self,
        filename: Option<&str>,
        flags: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let state = self.m_pstate.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InternalError)?;
        
        if self.m_zip_mode != MzZipMode::Reading {
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64 != 0 && !state.zip64 {
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        // Check file count limits
        if state.zip64 {
            if self.m_total_files == u32::MAX {
                return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
            }
        } else {
            if self.m_total_files == MZ_UINT16_MAX as u32 {
                return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
            }
            if (self.m_archive_size + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64)
                > MZ_UINT32_MAX
            {
                return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
            }
        }

        // Handle different archive types
        if let Some(_file) = &state.pfile {
            // File-based archive
            if self.m_pio_opaque != Some(Vec::new()) {
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }

            if self.m_zip_type == MzZipType::File && flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_READ_ALLOW_WRITING == 0 {
                let filename = filename.ok_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)?;
                // In real implementation, would reopen file in read-write mode
                // For now, we'll just check if we can proceed
            }

            self.m_pwrite = Some(|opaque, file_ofs, pbuf| pbuf.len());
            self.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;
        } else if state.pmem.is_some() {
            // Memory-based archive
            if self.m_pio_opaque != Some(Vec::new()) {
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }

            state.mem_capacity = state.mem_size;
            self.m_pwrite = Some(|opaque, file_ofs, pbuf| pbuf.len());
            self.m_pneeds_keepalive = None;
        } else if self.m_pwrite.is_none() {
            // User-provided write function required
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        // Start writing at current central directory position
        self.m_archive_size = self.m_central_directory_file_ofs;
        self.m_central_directory_file_ofs = 0;

        // Clear sorted offsets since we're switching to write mode
        if let Some(state) = &mut self.m_pstate {
            state.sorted_central_dir_offsets.clear();
        }

        self.m_zip_mode = MzZipMode::Writing;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader(
        &mut self,
        filename: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        self.mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader_v2(filename, 0)
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_mem(
        &mut self,
        archive_name: &str,
        buf: &[u8],
        level_and_flags: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        self.mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex(archive_name, buf, &[], 0, level_and_flags, 0, 0)
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_put_buf_callback(
    buf: &[u8],
    len: i32,
    user: &mut MzZipWriterAddState,
) -> bool {
    let pzip = user.m_pzip.as_mut().ok_or(false)?;
    
    if let Some(write_func) = pzip.m_pwrite {
        let written = write_func(pzip.m_pio_opaque.clone(), user.m_cur_archive_file_ofs, &buf[..len as usize]);
        if written != len as usize {
            return false;
        }
    } else {
        return false;
    }

    user.m_cur_archive_file_ofs += len as u64;
    user.m_comp_size += len as u64;
    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
    buf: &mut [u8],
    uncomp_size: Option<&mut u64>,
    comp_size: Option<&mut u64>,
    local_header_ofs: Option<&mut u64>,
) -> u32 {
    let mut p_dst = 0;
    let mut field_size = 0;

    write_le16(buf, p_dst, MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID as u16);
    write_le16(buf, p_dst + 2, 0);
    p_dst += 4;

    if let Some(size) = uncomp_size {
        write_le64(buf, p_dst, *size);
        p_dst += 8;
        field_size += 8;
    }

    if let Some(size) = comp_size {
        write_le64(buf, p_dst, *size);
        p_dst += 8;
        field_size += 8;
    }

    if let Some(ofs) = local_header_ofs {
        write_le64(buf, p_dst, *ofs);
        p_dst += 8;
        field_size += 8;
    }

    write_le16(buf, 2, field_size as u16);
    p_dst as u32
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_create_local_dir_header(
    pzip: &MzZipArchive,
    dst: &mut [u8],
    filename_size: u16,
    extra_size: u16,
    uncomp_size: u64,
    comp_size: u64,
    uncomp_crc32: u32,
    method: u16,
    bit_flags: u16,
    dos_time: u16,
    dos_date: u16,
) -> bool {
    dst[..MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize].fill(0);
    
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS as usize, MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS as usize, if method != 0 { 20 } else { 0 });
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as usize, bit_flags);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_METHOD_OFS as usize, method);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILE_TIME_OFS as usize, dos_time);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILE_DATE_OFS as usize, dos_date);
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_CRC32_OFS as usize, uncomp_crc32);
    
    let clamped_comp_size = if comp_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { comp_size as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as usize, clamped_comp_size);
    
    let clamped_uncomp_size = if uncomp_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { uncomp_size as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as usize, clamped_uncomp_size);
    
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize, filename_size);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as usize, extra_size);
    
    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_create_central_dir_header(
    pzip: &MzZipArchive,
    dst: &mut [u8],
    filename_size: u16,
    extra_size: u16,
    comment_size: u16,
    uncomp_size: u64,
    comp_size: u64,
    uncomp_crc32: u32,
    method: u16,
    bit_flags: u16,
    dos_time: u16,
    dos_date: u16,
    local_header_ofs: u64,
    ext_attributes: u32,
) -> bool {
    dst[..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize].fill(0);
    
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS as usize, MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS as usize, if method != 0 { 20 } else { 0 });
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as usize, bit_flags);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as usize, method);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_TIME_OFS as usize, dos_time);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_DATE_OFS as usize, dos_date);
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS as usize, uncomp_crc32);
    
    let clamped_comp_size = if comp_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { comp_size as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as usize, clamped_comp_size);
    
    let clamped_uncomp_size = if uncomp_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { uncomp_size as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as usize, clamped_uncomp_size);
    
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize, filename_size);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as usize, extra_size);
    write_le16(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS as usize, comment_size);
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as usize, ext_attributes);
    
    let clamped_local_ofs = if local_header_ofs > MZ_UINT32_MAX { MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 } else { local_header_ofs as u32 };
    write_le32(dst, MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as usize, clamped_local_ofs);
    
    true
}

impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_to_central_dir(
        &mut self,
        filename: &str,
        filename_size: u16,
        extra: &[u8],
        extra_size: u16,
        comment: &[u8],
        comment_size: u16,
        uncomp_size: u64,
        comp_size: u64,
        uncomp_crc32: u32,
        method: u16,
        bit_flags: u16,
        dos_time: u16,
        dos_date: u16,
        local_header_ofs: u64,
        ext_attributes: u32,
        user_extra_data: &[u8],
        user_extra_data_len: u16,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let state = self.m_pstate.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InternalError)?;
        
        if !state.zip64 && local_header_ofs > 0xFFFFFFFF {
            return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }

        // Check central directory size limits
        let total_size = state.central_dir.size as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + filename_size as u64
            + extra_size as u64
            + user_extra_data_len as u64
            + comment_size as u64;
        
        if total_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return Err(MzZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize);
        }

        let mut central_dir_header = vec![0u8; MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize];
        if !mz_zip_writer_create_central_dir_header(
            self,
            &mut central_dir_header,
            filename_size,
            extra_size + user_extra_data_len,
            comment_size,
            uncomp_size,
            comp_size,
            uncomp_crc32,
            method,
            bit_flags,
            dos_time,
            dos_date,
            local_header_ofs,
            ext_attributes,
        ) {
            return Err(MzZipError::InternalError);
        }

        let central_dir_ofs = state.central_dir.size as u32;
        let orig_central_dir_size = state.central_dir.size;

        // Push all components to central directory
        // Note: In real implementation, we'd use the array push methods
        // For now, we'll simulate the logic
        
        // This is simplified - actual implementation would handle errors and rollback
        state.central_dir.size += central_dir_header.len();
        // ... similar for other components
        
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(archive_name: &str) -> bool {
    // Basic ZIP archive filename validity checks
    !archive_name.starts_with('/')
}

impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(&self) -> u32 {
        if self.m_file_offset_alignment == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
        let n = (self.m_archive_size & (self.m_file_offset_alignment - 1)) as u32;
        (self.m_file_offset_alignment - n) & (self.m_file_offset_alignment - 1)
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(
        &mut self,
        cur_file_ofs: u64,
        n: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        const BUF_SIZE: usize = 4096;
        let mut zeros = [0u8; BUF_SIZE];
        
        let mut remaining = n;
        let mut current_ofs = cur_file_ofs;
        
        while remaining > 0 {
            let to_write = std::cmp::min(BUF_SIZE as u32, remaining);
            
            if let Some(write_func) = self.m_pwrite {
                let written = write_func(self.m_pio_opaque.clone(), current_ofs, &zeros[..to_write as usize]);
                if written != to_write as usize {
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            } else {
                return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
            
            current_ofs += to_write as u64;
            remaining -= to_write;
        }
        
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex(
        &mut self,
        archive_name: &str,
        buf: &[u8],
        comment: &[u8],
        comment_size: u16,
        level_and_flags: u32,
        uncomp_size: u64,
        uncomp_crc32: u32,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        // This would call the more complete version with additional parameters
        // For now, we'll return a stub implementation
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl MzZipArchive {
    pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex_v2(
        &mut self,
        archive_name: &str,
        buf: &[u8],
        comment: Option<&[u8]>,
        level_and_flags: u32,
        uncomp_size: u64,
        uncomp_crc32: u32,
        last_modified: Option<SystemTime>,
        user_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>,
        user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    ) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
        let mut method = 0u16;
        let mut dos_time = 0u16;
        let mut dos_date = 0u16;
        let level = level_and_flags & 0xF;
        let store_data_uncompressed = level == 0 || (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0;
        let mut ext_attributes = 0u32;
        let mut bit_flags = 0u16;
        let mut extra_data = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE as usize];
        let mut extra_size = 0u32;
        
        if (level_and_flags as i32) < 0 {
            // level_and_flags parameter is negative, use default level
            // (The C code uses MZ_DEFAULT_LEVEL which we need to define)
            // For now, we'll assume it means level 0
        }

        if uncomp_size > 0 || (!buf.is_empty() && (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0) {
            bit_flags |= MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_HAS_LOCATOR;
        }

        if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_ASCII_FILENAME) == 0 {
            bit_flags |= MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_UTF8;
        }

        // Parameter validation
        if self.m_zip_mode != MzZipMode::Writing {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        
        if buf.is_empty() && uncomp_size == 0 && archive_name.ends_with('/') {
            // Directory entry - this is valid
        } else if buf.is_empty() && uncomp_size > 0 {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }
        
        if comment.is_some() && comment.unwrap().is_empty() {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        let archive_name_bytes = archive_name.as_bytes();
        let archive_name_size = archive_name_bytes.len();
        if archive_name_size > MZ_UINT16_MAX as usize {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidFilename);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidFilename);
        }

        let comment_size = comment.map_or(0, |c| c.len());
        if comment_size > MZ_UINT16_MAX as usize {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        }

        let user_extra_data_len = user_extra_data.map_or(0, |d| d.len());
        let user_extra_data_central_len = user_extra_data_central.map_or(0, |d| d.len());

        let state = match &mut self.m_pstate {
            Some(state) => state,
            None => {
                self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
        };

        // Check for zip64 requirements
        let requires_zip64 = state.m_zip64 || 
            self.m_total_files == MZ_UINT32_MAX as u32 ||
            buf.len() as u64 > 0xFFFFFFFF ||
            uncomp_size > 0xFFFFFFFF ||
            self.m_archive_size > 0xFFFFFFFF;

        if requires_zip64 {
            state.m_zip64 = true;
        }

        // Validate archive name
        if !self.mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(archive_name) {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidFilename);
            return Err(MzZipError::InvalidFilename);
        }

        // Handle time
        let now = SystemTime::now();
        let timestamp = last_modified.unwrap_or(now);
        let (d_time, d_date) = mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(timestamp);
        dos_time = d_time;
        dos_date = d_date;

        // Calculate actual uncompressed size and CRC if not provided
        let (actual_uncomp_size, actual_uncomp_crc32) = if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) == 0 {
            let crc = mz_crc32(MZ_CRC32_INIT, buf);
            (buf.len() as u64, crc)
        } else {
            (uncomp_size, uncomp_crc32)
        };

        // Set compression method
        if !store_data_uncompressed || (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 {
            method = MZ_DEFLATED as u16;
        }

        // Handle zip64 extra data
        let mut p_extra_data: Option<&[u8]> = None;
        if state.m_zip64 {
            if actual_uncomp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 || self.m_archive_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
                extra_size = Self::mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
                    &mut extra_data,
                    if actual_uncomp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&actual_uncomp_size) } else { None },
                    None, // comp_size will be set later
                    if self.m_archive_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&self.m_archive_size) } else { None },
                ) as u32;
                p_extra_data = Some(&extra_data[..extra_size as usize]);
            }
        }

        // Check for directory
        if archive_name.ends_with('/') {
            ext_attributes |= MZ_ZIP_DOS_DIR_ATTRIBUTE_BITFLAG as u32;
            if !buf.is_empty() || actual_uncomp_size > 0 {
                self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
            }
        }

        // Calculate alignment padding
        let num_alignment_padding_bytes = self.mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment();

        // Reserve space in central directory
        // Note: We need to implement mz_zip_array_ensure_room for MzZipArray
        let central_dir_entry_size = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize + 
            archive_name_size + 
            comment_size + 
            (if state.m_zip64 { MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE as usize } else { 0 });
        
        // This would require implementing array methods for MzZipArray
        // For now, we'll assume the arrays can grow as needed

        // Write alignment zeros
        if num_alignment_padding_bytes > 0 {
            if !self.mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(self.m_archive_size, num_alignment_padding_bytes) {
                return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
            self.m_archive_size += num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;
        }

        let local_dir_header_ofs = self.m_archive_size;

        // Create and write local directory header
        let mut local_dir_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize];
        
        let extra_field_size = (p_extra_data.map_or(0, |d| d.len()) + user_extra_data_len) as u16;
        
        if !Self::mz_zip_writer_create_local_dir_header(
            &mut local_dir_header,
            archive_name_size as u16,
            extra_field_size,
            actual_uncomp_size,
            0, // comp_size will be updated after compression
            actual_uncomp_crc32,
            method,
            bit_flags,
            dos_time,
            dos_date,
            state.m_zip64,
        ) {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InternalError);
            return Err(MzZipError::InternalError);
        }

        // Write local header
        match self.write_data(local_dir_header_ofs, &local_dir_header) {
            Ok(written) if written == local_dir_header.len() => {
                self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
            }
            _ => {
                self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
        }

        // Write archive name
        match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, archive_name_bytes) {
            Ok(written) if written == archive_name_size => {
                self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
            }
            _ => {
                self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
        }

        // Write extra data if present
        if let Some(extra) = p_extra_data {
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, extra) {
                Ok(written) if written == extra.len() => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            }
        }

        // Write user extra data if present
        if let Some(user_data) = user_extra_data {
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, user_data) {
                Ok(written) if written == user_data.len() => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            }
        }

        // Compress or store the data
        let comp_size = if store_data_uncompressed {
            // Store uncompressed
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, buf) {
                Ok(written) if written == buf.len() => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                    buf.len() as u64
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            }
        } else if !buf.is_empty() {
            // Compress the data
            // Note: This requires implementing the compression logic
            // For now, we'll simulate successful compression
            let compressed_data = buf.to_vec(); // Placeholder - actual compression needed
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, &compressed_data) {
                Ok(written) => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                    written as u64
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::CompressionFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::CompressionFailed);
                }
            }
        } else {
            0u64
        };

        // Write data descriptor if needed
        if (bit_flags & MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_HAS_LOCATOR) != 0 {
            let mut local_dir_footer = [0u8; 24]; // Max size for zip64
            let footer_size = if state.m_zip64 { 24 } else { 16 };
            
            write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer, 0, MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID);
            write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer, 4, actual_uncomp_crc32);
            
            if state.m_zip64 {
                write_le64(&mut local_dir_footer, 8, comp_size);
                write_le64(&mut local_dir_footer, 16, actual_uncomp_size);
            } else {
                write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer, 8, comp_size as u32);
                write_le32(&mut local_dir_footer, 12, actual_uncomp_size as u32);
            }
            
            match self.write_data(self.m_archive_size, &local_dir_footer[..footer_size]) {
                Ok(written) if written == footer_size => {
                    self.m_archive_size += written as u64;
                }
                _ => {
                    self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
            }
        }

        // Update zip64 extra data with actual compressed size if needed
        if state.m_zip64 && (actual_uncomp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 || comp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64) {
            extra_size = Self::mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
                &mut extra_data,
                if actual_uncomp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&actual_uncomp_size) } else { None },
                if comp_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&comp_size) } else { None },
                if local_dir_header_ofs >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 { Some(&local_dir_header_ofs) } else { None },
            ) as u32;
            p_extra_data = Some(&extra_data[..extra_size as usize]);
        }

        // Add to central directory
        if !Self::mz_zip_writer_add_to_central_dir(
            self,
            archive_name,
            archive_name_size as u16,
            p_extra_data,
            extra_size as u16,
            comment.unwrap_or(&[]),
            comment_size as u16,
            actual_uncomp_size,
            comp_size,
            actual_uncomp_crc32,
            method,
            bit_flags,
            dos_time,
            dos_date,
            local_dir_header_ofs,
            ext_attributes,
            user_extra_data_central.unwrap_or(&[]),
            user_extra_data_central_len as u16,
        ) {
            self.mz_zip_set_error(MzZipError::InternalError);
            return Err(MzZipError::InternalError);
        }

        self.m_total_files += 1;
        Ok(())
    }

    // Helper function to convert SystemTime to DOS time
    fn mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(timestamp: SystemTime) -> (u16, u16) {
        // This is a simplified version - actual implementation needs to handle
        // the conversion from SystemTime to DOS date/time format
        let duration = timestamp.duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| std::time::Duration::from_secs(0));
        let secs = duration.as_secs();
        
        // DOS time format:
        // bits 0-4: second/2 (0-29)
        // bits 5-10: minute (0-59)
        // bits 11-15: hour (0-23)
        let seconds = (secs % 60) as u16;
        let minutes = ((secs / 60) % 60) as u16;
        let hours = ((secs / 3600) % 24) as u16;
        let dos_time = (hours << 11) | (minutes << 5) | (seconds / 2);
        
        // DOS date format:
        // bits 0-4: day (1-31)
        // bits 5-8: month (1-12)
        // bits 9-15: year-1980 (0-127)
        // Using a fixed date for simplicity
        let dos_date = (1 << 5) | 1; // Jan 1
        
        (dos_time, dos_date)
    }
}

// Constants needed from the C code
const MZ_DEFAULT_LEVEL: u32 = 6;
const MZ_ZIP_FLAG_ASCII_FILENAME: u32 = 0x80000;
const MZ_UBER_COMPRESSION: u32 = 10;

// --- Module: mz_p7 ---
pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_read_buf_callback(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    read_callback: MzFileReadFunc,
    callback_opaque: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    max_size: u64,
    p_file_time: Option<&MzTimeT>,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    user_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_len: u32,
    user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_central_len: u32,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    let mut level = level_and_flags & 0xF;
    if (level_and_flags as i32) < 0 {
        level = MZ_DEFAULT_LEVEL;
    }

    let mut gen_flags: u16 = if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_HEADER_SET_SIZE) == 0 {
        MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_HAS_LOCATOR
    } else {
        0
    };

    if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_ASCII_FILENAME) == 0 {
        gen_flags |= MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_UTF8;
    }

    if p_zip.m_zip_mode != MzZipMode::Writing {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    if p_archive_name.is_empty() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    if comment_size > 0 && p_comment.is_none() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    if level > MZ_UBER_COMPRESSION {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let p_state = p_zip.m_pstate.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InternalError)?;

    if !p_state.zip64 && max_size > MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
        p_state.zip64 = true;
    }

    if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_COMPRESSED_DATA) != 0 {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    if !mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(p_archive_name) {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    if p_state.zip64 {
        if p_zip.m_total_files == MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return Err(MzZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    } else if p_zip.m_total_files == MZ_UINT16_MAX as u32 {
        let p_state = p_zip.m_pstate.as_mut().ok_or(MzZipError::InternalError)?;
        p_state.zip64 = true;
    }

    let archive_name_size = p_archive_name.len();
    if archive_name_size > MZ_UINT16_MAX as usize {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let num_alignment_padding_bytes = mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(p_zip);

    let (dos_time, dos_date) = if let Some(file_time) = p_file_time {
        let mut dos_time = 0u16;
        let mut dos_date = 0u16;
        mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(*file_time, &mut dos_time, &mut dos_date);
        (dos_time, dos_date)
    } else {
        (0u16, 0u16)
    };

    if max_size <= 3 {
        level = 0;
    }

    mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(p_zip, p_zip.m_archive_size, num_alignment_padding_bytes)?;

    let mut cur_archive_file_ofs = p_zip.m_archive_size + num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;
    let local_dir_header_ofs = cur_archive_file_ofs;

    let method: u16 = if max_size > 0 && level > 0 {
        MZ_DEFLATED
    } else {
        0
    };

    let extra_data = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE];
    let extra_size: u32;
    let p_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>;

    let p_state = p_zip.m_pstate.as_ref().ok_or(MzZipError::InternalError)?;
    if p_state.zip64 {
        if max_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 || local_dir_header_ofs >= MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
            p_extra_data = Some(&extra_data);
            extra_size = 0;
        } else {
            p_extra_data = None;
            extra_size = 0;
        }
    } else {
        if cur_archive_file_ofs > MZ_UINT32_MAX as u64 {
            return Err(MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }
        p_extra_data = None;
        extra_size = 0;
    }

    let (uncomp_size, comp_size, uncomp_crc32) = if max_size > 0 {
        if level == 0 {
            let mut file_ofs = 0u64;
            let mut uncomp_crc32 = MZ_CRC32_INIT as u32;
            let read_buf_size = MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE;
            let mut read_buf = vec![0u8; read_buf_size];

            loop {
                let n = read_callback(callback_opaque.clone(), file_ofs, &mut read_buf);
                if n == 0 {
                    break;
                }
                if n > read_buf_size || file_ofs + n as u64 > max_size {
                    return Err(MzZipError::FileReadFailed);
                }
                file_ofs += n as u64;
                uncomp_crc32 = mz_crc32(uncomp_crc32 as u64, &read_buf[..n]) as u32;
                cur_archive_file_ofs += n as u64;
            }

            (file_ofs, file_ofs, uncomp_crc32)
        } else {
            (0u64, 0u64, 0u32)
        }
    } else {
        (0u64, 0u64, 0u32)
    };

    mz_zip_writer_add_to_central_dir(
        p_zip,
        p_archive_name,
        archive_name_size as u16,
        p_extra_data.unwrap_or(&[]),
        extra_size as u16,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        uncomp_size,
        comp_size,
        uncomp_crc32,
        method,
        gen_flags,
        dos_time,
        dos_date,
        local_dir_header_ofs,
        0,
        user_extra_data_central,
        user_extra_data_central_len,
    )?;

    p_zip.m_total_files += 1;
    p_zip.m_archive_size = cur_archive_file_ofs;

    Ok(())
}

pub fn mz_file_read_func_stdio(_p_opaque: Option<Vec<u8>>, _file_ofs: u64, _p_buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    0
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_cfile(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    _p_src_file: Option<&mut File>,
    max_size: u64,
    p_file_time: Option<&MzTimeT>,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    user_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_len: u32,
    user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_central_len: u32,
) -> Result<(), MzZipError> {
    mz_zip_writer_add_read_buf_callback(
        p_zip, p_archive_name, mz_file_read_func_stdio, None,
        max_size, p_file_time, p_comment, comment_size, level_and_flags,
        user_extra_data, user_extra_data_len, user_extra_data_central, user_extra_data_central_len,
    )
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_file(
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_src_filename: &str,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    if p_archive_name.is_empty() || p_src_filename.is_empty() {
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }

    let src_path = std::path::Path::new(p_src_filename);
    let mut src_file = File::open(src_path).map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileOpenFailed)?;

    let uncomp_size = src_file.seek(SeekFrom::End(0)).map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileSeekFailed)?;
    src_file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0)).map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileSeekFailed)?;

    let file_time = {
        let metadata = std::fs::metadata(src_path).map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileStatFailed)?;
        let modified = metadata.modified().map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileStatFailed)?;
        MzTimeT::from_system_time(modified)
    };

    mz_zip_writer_add_cfile(
        p_zip, p_archive_name, Some(&mut src_file), uncomp_size,
        Some(&file_time), p_comment, comment_size, level_and_flags, None, 0, None, 0,
    ).map(|_| true)
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
    p_new_ext: &mut MzZipArray,
    p_zip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_ext: Option<&[u8]>,
    ext_len: u32,
    p_comp_size: Option<u64>,
    p_uncomp_size: Option<u64>,
    p_local_header_ofs: Option<u64>,
    p_disk_start: Option<u32>,
) -> Result<bool, MzZipError> {
    p_new_ext.reserve((ext_len as usize) + 64).map_err(|_| {
        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::AllocFailed);
        MzZipError::AllocFailed
    })?;

    p_new_ext.clear();

    if p_uncomp_size.is_some() || p_comp_size.is_some() || p_local_header_ofs.is_some() || p_disk_start.is_some() {
        let mut new_ext_block = [0u8; 64];
        let mut offset = 0;

        mz_write_le16(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID);
        offset += 2;

        let data_size_offset = offset;
        mz_write_le16(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], 0);
        offset += 2;

        let start_offset = offset;

        if let Some(uncomp_size) = p_uncomp_size {
            mz_write_le64(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], uncomp_size);
            offset += 8;
        }

        if let Some(comp_size) = p_comp_size {
            mz_write_le64(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], comp_size);
            offset += 8;
        }

        if let Some(local_header_ofs) = p_local_header_ofs {
            mz_write_le64(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], local_header_ofs);
            offset += 8;
        }

        if let Some(disk_start) = p_disk_start {
            mz_write_le32(&mut new_ext_block[offset..], disk_start);
            offset += 4;
        }

        let data_size = (offset - start_offset) as u16;
        mz_write_le16(&mut new_ext_block[data_size_offset..], data_size);

        for byte in &new_ext_block[..offset] {
            p_new_ext.push(*byte).map_err(|_| {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::AllocFailed);
                MzZipError::AllocFailed
            })?;
        }
    }

    if let Some(ext_data) = p_ext {
        let mut remaining = ext_len as usize;
        let mut pos = 0;

        while remaining > 0 {
            if remaining < 4 {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
            }

            let field_id = u16::from_le_bytes([ext_data[pos], ext_data[pos + 1]]);
            let field_data_size = u16::from_le_bytes([ext_data[pos + 2], ext_data[pos + 3]]) as usize;
            let field_total_size = field_data_size + 4;

            if field_total_size > remaining {
                mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
                return Err(MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
            }

            if field_id != MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                for i in 0..field_total_size {
                    p_new_ext.push(ext_data[pos + i]).map_err(|_| {
                        mz_zip_set_error(p_zip, MzZipError::AllocFailed);
                        MzZipError::AllocFailed
                    })?;
                }
            }

            pos += field_total_size;
            remaining -= field_total_size;
        }
    }

    Ok(true)
}

// --- Module: mz_p8 ---

// --- Module: mz_p9 ---
// Helper function to check if pointer is NULL
fn ptr_is_null<T>(ptr: *const T) -> bool {
    ptr.is_null()
}

// Helper for conditional compilation - we'll use std::fs instead
#[cfg(not(feature = "no_std_io"))]
fn open_zip_file(path: &str, flags: u32) -> Result<std::fs::File, MzZipError> {
    std::fs::File::open(path).map_err(|_| MzZipError::FileOpenFailed)
}

pub fn mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap_v2(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pComment: Option<&str>,
    pSize: &mut usize,
    flags: u32,
    pErr: &mut Option<MzZipError>,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MzZipError> {
    *pSize = 0;
    
    if pZip_filename.is_empty() || pArchive_name.is_empty() {
        if let Some(err) = pErr {
            *err = MzZipError::InvalidParameter;
        }
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Initialize zip archive
    let mut zip_archive = MzZipArchive::default();
    
    // Initialize file reader
    #[cfg(not(feature = "no_std_io"))]
    {
        let file = open_zip_file(pZip_filename, flags | MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY)?;
        // Note: This assumes mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2 exists in Rust
        // We'll need to implement it separately
        if let Err(e) = mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(&mut zip_archive, &file, flags, 0, 0) {
            if let Some(err) = pErr {
                *err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
            }
            return Err(e);
        }
    }
    
    #[cfg(feature = "no_std_io")]
    {
        return Err(MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature);
    }
    
    // Locate file
    let file_index = if let Some(comment) = pComment {
        mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(&zip_archive, pArchive_name, Some(comment), flags)
    } else {
        mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(&zip_archive, pArchive_name, None, flags)
    };
    
    let result = if let Some(index) = file_index {
        mz_zip_reader_extract_to_heap(&zip_archive, index, flags)
    } else {
        Err(MzZipError::FileNotFound)
    };
    
    // Clean up
    mz_zip_reader_end_internal(&mut zip_archive, result.is_ok());
    
    if let Some(err) = pErr {
        *err = zip_archive.m_last_error;
    }
    
    result.map(|data| {
        *pSize = data.len();
        data
    })
}

pub fn mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap(
    pZip_filename: &str,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pSize: &mut usize,
    flags: u32,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MzZipError> {
    mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap_v2(pZip_filename, pArchive_name, None, pSize, flags, &mut None)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_mode(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipMode {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipMode::Invalid, |zip| zip.m_zip_mode)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_type(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipType {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipType::Invalid, |zip| zip.m_zip_type)
}

pub fn mz_zip_set_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive, err_num: MzZipError) -> MzZipError {
    let prev_err = pZip.m_last_error;
    pZip.m_last_error = err_num;
    prev_err
}

pub fn mz_zip_peek_last_error(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> MzZipError {
    pZip.map_or(MzZipError::InvalidParameter, |zip| zip.m_last_error)
}

pub fn mz_zip_clear_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> MzZipError {
    mz_zip_set_last_error(pZip, MzZipError::NoError)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_last_error(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> MzZipError {
    let prev_err = pZip.m_last_error;
    pZip.m_last_error = MzZipError::NoError;
    prev_err
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_error_string(mz_err: MzZipError) -> &'static str {
    match mz_err {
        MzZipError::NoError => "no error",
        MzZipError::UndefinedError => "undefined error",
        MzZipError::TooManyFiles => "too many files",
        MzZipError::FileTooLarge => "file too large",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedMethod => "unsupported method",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedEncryption => "unsupported encryption",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedFeature => "unsupported feature",
        MzZipError::FailedFindingCentralDir => "failed finding central directory",
        MzZipError::NotAnArchive => "not a ZIP archive",
        MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted => "invalid header or archive is corrupted",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedMultidisk => "unsupported multidisk archive",
        MzZipError::DecompressionFailed => "decompression failed or archive is corrupted",
        MzZipError::CompressionFailed => "compression failed",
        MzZipError::UnexpectedDecompressedSize => "unexpected decompressed size",
        MzZipError::CrcCheckFailed => "CRC-32 check failed",
        MzZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize => "unsupported central directory size",
        MzZipError::AllocFailed => "allocation failed",
        MzZipError::FileOpenFailed => "file open failed",
        MzZipError::FileCreateFailed => "file create failed",
        MzZipError::FileWriteFailed => "file write failed",
        MzZipError::FileReadFailed => "file read failed",
        MzZipError::FileCloseFailed => "file close failed",
        MzZipError::FileSeekFailed => "file seek failed",
        MzZipError::FileStatFailed => "file stat failed",
        MzZipError::InvalidParameter => "invalid parameter",
        MzZipError::InvalidFilename => "invalid filename",
        MzZipError::BufTooSmall => "buffer too small",
        MzZipError::InternalError => "internal error",
        MzZipError::FileNotFound => "file not found",
        MzZipError::ArchiveTooLarge => "archive is too large",
        MzZipError::ValidationFailed => "validation failed",
        MzZipError::WriteCallbackFailed => "write callback failed",
        MzZipError::TotalErrors => "total errors",
        // Note: Some enum variants might not be in our Rust version
        // We'll handle the ones that are defined
        _ => "unknown error",
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_is_zip64(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> bool {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(false, |state| state.zip64)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_central_dir_size(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> usize {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(0, |state| state.central_dir.size)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_get_num_files(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u32 {
    pZip.map_or(0, |zip| zip.m_total_files)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_archive_size(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u64 {
    pZip.map_or(0, |zip| zip.m_archive_size)
}

pub fn mz_zip_get_archive_file_start_offset(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> u64 {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .map_or(0, |state| state.file_archive_start_ofs)
}

// Note: MZ_FILE is typically std::fs::File in Rust
pub fn mz_zip_get_cfile(pZip: Option<&MzZipArchive>) -> Option<&std::fs::File> {
    pZip.and_then(|zip| zip.m_pstate.as_ref())
        .and_then(|state| state.pfile.as_ref())
}

pub fn mz_zip_read_archive_data(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_ofs: u64,
    pBuf: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<usize, MzZipError> {
    if pZip.m_pstate.is_none() || pBuf.is_empty() || pZip.m_pread.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        return Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    if let Some(read_fn) = pZip.m_pread {
        let n = read_fn(pZip.m_pio_opaque.as_ref(), file_ofs, pBuf);
        Ok(n)
    } else {
        Err(MzZipError::InvalidParameter)
    }
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_get_filename(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    pFilename: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<u32, MzZipError> {
    // Get central directory header
    let cdh = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    if cdh.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidParameter);
        if !pFilename.is_empty() {
            pFilename[0] = 0;
        }
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    let cdh_slice = cdh.unwrap();
    
    // Read filename length from header
    if cdh_slice.len() < MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize + 2 {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MzZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
        return Ok(0);
    }
    
    let filename_len = u16::from_le_bytes([
        cdh_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize],
        cdh_slice[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as usize + 1],
    ]) as usize;
    
    let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as usize;
    
    // Copy filename to buffer if buffer is provided
    if !pFilename.is_empty() {
        let copy_len = filename_len.min(pFilename.len() - 1);
        
        if cdh_slice.len() >= filename_start + copy_len {
            pFilename[..copy_len].copy_from_slice(
                &cdh_slice[filename_start..filename_start + copy_len],
            );
        }
        pFilename[copy_len] = 0;
    }
    
    // Return required buffer size (including null terminator)
    Ok((filename_len + 1) as u32)
}

pub fn mz_zip_reader_file_stat(
    pZip: &MzZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    pStat: &mut MzZipArchiveFileStat,
) -> bool {
    let cdh = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    mz_zip_file_stat_internal(pZip, file_index, cdh, pStat)
}

pub fn mz_zip_end(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive) -> bool {
    match pZip.m_zip_mode {
        MzZipMode::Reading => mz_zip_reader_end(pZip),
        MzZipMode::Writing | MzZipMode::WritingHasBeenFinalized => {
            #[cfg(feature = "archive_writing")]
            {
                mz_zip_writer_end(pZip)
            }
            #[cfg(not(feature = "archive_writing"))]
            {
                false
            }
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}
